    Vigraha
    दुष्टः आत्मा यस्य सः, तस्मिन् duṣṭaḥ ātmā yasya saḥ, tasmin
    Literally
    “one whose self is bad”
    In the verse
    the wicked one

  • सुरारिबले surāribale

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (सुरारि)

    Members
    सुरारि + बल
    Vigraha
    सुराणाम् अरयः सुरारयः; सुरारीणां बलं सुरारिबलम्, तस्मिन् surāṇām arayaḥ surārayaḥ; surārīṇāṃ balaṃ surāribalam, tasmin
    Literally
    “in the army of the enemies of the gods”
    In the verse
    Mahiṣa’s asura host

  • प्रणतिनम्रशिरोधरांसाः praṇatinamraśirodharāṃsāḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing an itaretara dvandva (शिरोधरांसाः) and a tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (प्रणतिनम्र)

    Members
    प्रणति + नम्र + शिरोधरा + अंस
    Vigraha
    शिरोधराः च अंसाः च शिरोधरांसाः; प्रणत्या नम्राः शिरोधरांसाः येषां ते śirodharāḥ ca aṃsāḥ ca śirodharāṃsāḥ; praṇatyā namrāḥ śirodharāṃsāḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “whose necks and shoulders are bent by obeisance”
    In the verse
    bowing low as they praised
    Alternative
    शिरोधरा ‘neck’ is itself a compound, शिरः धरति इति ‘that which holds the head’ (upapada); the whole is still one bahuvrīhi.

  • प्रहर्षपुलकोद्गमचारुदेहाः praharṣapulakodgamacārudehāḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing tatpuruṣas (ṣaṣṭhī पुलकोद्गम; tṛtīyā प्रहर्षपुलकोद्गम)

    Members
    प्रहर्ष + पुलकोद्गम + चारु + देह
    Vigraha
    पुलकानाम् उद्गमः पुलकोद्गमः; प्रहर्षेण (जातः) पुलकोद्गमः प्रहर्षपुलकोद्गमः; तेन चारवः देहाः येषां ते pulakānām udgamaḥ pulakodgamaḥ; praharṣeṇa (jātaḥ) pulakodgamaḥ praharṣapulakodgamaḥ; tena cāravaḥ dehāḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally
    “whose bodies are beautiful with the rising of hair-thrills from great joy”
    In the verse
    thrilling with joy
Morphology & anvaya

  • तुष्टुवुः tuṣṭuvuḥ

    Perfect of √स्तु: reduplication तु-स्तु-, the root’s s becomes ṣ after the u of the reduplicated syllable and the t then becomes ṭ beside it (ष्टुना ष्टुः, 8.4.41); before the vowel ending -उस् the root’s u becomes uv: तुष्टुवुः ‘they praised’.

  • निहते nihate

    Locative absolute: participle and noun in the locative (तस्मिन् … निहते) state the circumstance ‘when he had been slain’; the agent देव्या stands in the instrumental.

  • वाग्भिः vāgbhiḥ

    Instrumental plural of वाच्: before the ending -भिः the final c becomes k and is then voiced to g (झलां जशोऽन्ते, 8.2.39).

Anvaya · prose order

तस्मिन् अतिवीर्ये दुरात्मनि सुरारिबले च देव्या निहते, प्रणतिनम्रशिरोधरांसाः प्रहर्षपुलकोद्गमचारुदेहाः शक्रादयः सुरगणाः तां वाग्भिः तुष्टुवुः।

tasmin ativīrye durātmani surāribale ca devyā nihate, praṇatinamraśirodharāṃsāḥ praharṣapulakodgamacārudehāḥ śakrādayaḥ suragaṇāḥ tāṃ vāgbhiḥ tuṣṭuvuḥ |

A locative absolute (तस्मिन् … सुरारिबले च देव्या निहते) sets the time; the main clause is शक्रादयः सुरगणाः तां तुष्टुवुः, with two bahuvrīhi epithets of the gods and the instrument वाग्भिः.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The narrative frame of the hymn: Medhas tells how the gods praised the Devī after Mahiṣa’s death (3.42–44). With this stanza the text changes metre: 4.2–4.23 are in vasantatilakā, a fourteen-syllable quarter printed one quarter to a line, so a word can run over a line-end (as in 4.7–4.10); 4.24–4.27 return to anuṣṭubh.

Traditional interpretation

The chapter’s colophon names it शक्रादिस्तुतिः, ‘the praise by Śakra and the others’, after the first word of this stanza. The bodily signs — bowing and the thrill of joy (pulaka) — are the classical marks of devotion: the praise is offered by the whole person, not only in words.

Verse 4.3

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

देव्या यया ततमिदं जगदात्मशक्त्यानिश्शेषदेवगणशक्तिसमूहमूर्त्या।तामम्बिकामखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्यांभक्त्या नताः स्म विदधातु शुभानि सा नः॥⁠३⁠॥

devyā yayā tatamidaṃ jagadātmaśaktyāniśśeṣadevagaṇaśaktisamūhamūrtyā |tāmambikāmakhiladevamaharṣipūjyāṃbhaktyā natāḥ sma vidadhātu śubhāni sā naḥ ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

देव्या · यया · ततम् · इदम् · जगत् · आत्मशक्त्यानिश्शेषदेवगणशक्तिसमूहमूर्त्याताम् · अम्बिकाम् · अखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्याम्भक्त्या · नताः · स्म · विदधातु · शुभानि · सा · नः

devyā · yayā · tatam · idam · jagat · ātmaśaktyāniśśeṣadevagaṇaśaktisamūhamūrtyātām · ambikām · akhiladevamaharṣipūjyāmbhaktyā · natāḥ · sma · vidadhātu · śubhāni · sā · naḥ

Word by word

  1. देव्याdevyāby the Devīf. instr. sg. of देवी; agent of ततम्
  2. ययाyayāby whom, by whichrelative pronoun यद्, f. instr. sg.; agrees with देव्या
  3. ततम्tatamis pervaded, spread outpast passive participle (kta) of √तन् (tanādi, 8U ‘to stretch’), n. nom. sg.; predicate of जगत्
  4. इदम्idamthispronoun इदम्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies जगत्
  5. जगत्jagatworldn. nom. sg. of जगत्; subject of the passive ततम्
  6. आत्मशक्त्याātmaśaktyāby her own powerf. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); instrument
  7. निश्शेषदेवगणशक्तिसमूहमूर्त्याniśśeṣadevagaṇaśaktisamūhamūrtyāwhose form is the gathered powers of all the hosts of godsf. instr. sg. of the i-stem bahuvrīhi; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies देव्या
  8. ताम्tāmto thatpronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; correlative of यया; object of नताः
  9. अम्बिकाम्ambikāmAmbikā, the Motherf. acc. sg. of अम्बिका
  10. अखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्याम्akhiladevamaharṣipūjyāmworthy of worship by all gods and great sagesf. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अम्बिकाम्
  11. भक्त्याbhaktyāwith devotionf. instr. sg. of भक्ति; instrument of manner
  12. नताःnatāḥbowedpast participle (kta) of √नम् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to bow’), m. nom. pl.; the gods are the implied subject
  13. स्मsma(we) areas printed the indeclinable स्म; here generally understood as स्मः (√अस्, adādi 2P, laṭ, uttama-puruṣa bahuvacana ‘we are’) without its visarga, completing नताः: ‘we are bowed’ (see flags)
  14. विदधातुvidadhātumay she bestowवि + √धा (juhotyādi, 3U ‘to place’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  15. शुभानिśubhāniauspicious things, blessingsn. acc. pl. of शुभ; object of विदधातु
  16. साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of विदधातु
  17. नःnaḥon us, for usfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic dat. pl.

Translation

To that Ambikā, worthy of worship by all gods and great sages — the Devī, whose form is the mass of the powers of all the hosts of gods, by whom through her own power this world is pervaded — we are bowed with devotion. May she bestow auspicious things on us.

In readable EnglishTo Ambikā, worshipped by all the gods and great sages — the Devī who by her own power pervades this world, her form the gathered powers of all the hosts of gods — we bow in devotion. May she grant us what is good.

The passive ‘by whom … is pervaded’ is turned active; ‘we bow’ renders नताः स्म.

Textual verification / uncertain: Variant: निश्शेष (Gita Press, kept) against निःशेष (GRETIL, Freund) — orthographic only, same word. Interpretation: नताः स्म is taken in the usual sense ‘we are bowed’ (स्म for स्मः, the visarga absent, as the metre requires); it may also be the particle स्म with the bare participle (‘bowed indeed’). The sense is the same.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • ततमिदं जगदात्मशक्त्या tatamidaṃ jagadātmaśaktyā → ततम् + इदम् + जगत् + आत्मशक्त्या

    final m before vowel and consonant; consonant sandhi (voicing) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    ततम् keeps its m before the vowel of इदम्; इदम् shows anusvāra before j; the t of जगत् becomes d before the vowel ā.

  • निश्शेष niśśeṣa → निः + शेष

    visarga sandhi inside a compound · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    The visarga of निः becomes s before the sibilant and the s becomes ś beside ś: निश्शेष. Visarga before a sibilant may also simply stay (an optional rule): निःशेष, the reading of GRETIL and Freund (corrections.json, variant).

  • तामम्बिकामखिल tāmambikāmakhila → ताम् + अम्बिकाम् + अखिल…

    final m before a vowel · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    Both final m’s stand before vowels and are written together with them.

  • अखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्यां akhiladevamaharṣipūjyāṃ → अखिल + देव + महा + ऋषि + पूज्याम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    ā + ṛ → ar (महा + ऋषि → महर्षि). The final m becomes anusvāra before the bh of भक्त्या (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

  • नताः स्म natāḥ sma → नताः + स्म

    visarga before a sibilant · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)

    Before the s the visarga is kept (the optional alternative to assimilation).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • आत्मशक्त्या ātmaśaktyā

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    आत्मन् + शक्ति
    Vigraha
    आत्मनः शक्तिः, तया ātmanaḥ śaktiḥ, tayā
    Literally
    “by the power of (her) self”
    In the verse
    by her own power, needing nothing outside her

  • निश्शेषदेवगणशक्तिसमूहमूर्त्या niśśeṣadevagaṇaśaktisamūhamūrtyā

    bahuvrīhi containing ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣas (…शक्ति, …समूह) and a karmadhāraya (निश्शेषदेवगण)

    Members
    निश्शेष + देवगण + शक्ति + समूह + मूर्ति
    Vigraha
    निश्शेषाः देवगणाः; निश्शेषदेवगणानां शक्तयः; तासां समूहः; सः मूर्तिः यस्याः सा, तया niśśeṣāḥ devagaṇāḥ; niśśeṣadevagaṇānāṃ śaktayaḥ; tāsāṃ samūhaḥ; saḥ mūrtiḥ yasyāḥ sā, tayā
    Literally
    “whose form is the mass of the powers of the entire hosts of gods”
    In the verse
    the Devī whose body was formed from the gathered energies of all the gods (2.10–19)
    Alternative
    It can also be read as a karmadhāraya qualifying आत्मशक्त्या: ‘by her own power, which is the embodied mass of the gods’ powers’. The feminine i-stem ending fits either; the bahuvrīhi, describing the Devī herself, matches chapter 2.

  • निश्शेष niśśeṣa

    bahuvrīhi (prādi)

    Members
    निस् + शेष
    Vigraha
    निर्गतः शेषः यस्मात् तत् nirgataḥ śeṣaḥ yasmāt tat
    Literally
    “from which the remainder has gone”
    In the verse
    all, without exception

  • अखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्याम् akhiladevamaharṣipūjyām

    tṛtīyā tatpuruṣa (with the gerundive पूज्य) containing an itaretara dvandva (देवमहर्षि); महर्षि is a karmadhāraya (महान् ऋषिः)

    Members
    अखिल + देव + महर्षि + पूज्य
    Vigraha
    देवाः च महर्षयः च देवमहर्षयः; अखिलैः देवमहर्षिभिः पूज्या, ताम् devāḥ ca maharṣayaḥ ca devamaharṣayaḥ; akhilaiḥ devamaharṣibhiḥ pūjyā, tām
    Literally
    “to be worshipped by all the gods and great sages”
    In the verse
    honoured by every god and seer
Morphology & anvaya

  • ततम् tatam

    Past passive participle of √तन्: the root’s nasal is lost before the participle ending (तन् + त → तत). With no copula expressed, ततम् is a finite-like predicate: ‘(this world) is pervaded’.

  • विदधातु vidadhātu

    Imperative third singular of वि + √धा, a reduplicating (juhotyādi) root: दधा- + तु. ‘May she grant’ — the benediction that closes the first stanza.

  • स्म sma

    The metre (vasantatilakā, ⏑ in the fifth syllable) needs a light syllable here, so the printed स्म cannot be a sandhi form of स्मः (which would give स्मो before v). The same phrase returns in 4.5 (नताः स्म परिपालय).

Anvaya · prose order

यया निश्शेषदेवगणशक्तिसमूहमूर्त्या देव्या आत्मशक्त्या इदं जगत् ततम्, तां अखिलदेवमहर्षिपूज्याम् अम्बिकां (वयं) भक्त्या नताः स्म। सा नः शुभानि विदधातु।

yayā niśśeṣadevagaṇaśaktisamūhamūrtyā devyā ātmaśaktyā idaṃ jagat tatam, tāṃ akhiladevamaharṣipūjyām ambikāṃ (vayaṃ) bhaktyā natāḥ sma | sā naḥ śubhāni vidadhātu |

A relative clause (यया … ततम्) describes the Devī; the main clause (ताम् … नताः स्म) is the act of bowing; a separate benediction with the imperative विदधातु closes the stanza.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The first stanza of praise recalls how the Devī came to be: her body was formed from the tejas of all the gods (2.10–19). Yet the world is pervaded by her own power (आत्मशक्त्या): what the gods gave was not borrowed strength. The gods bow and ask for blessings (शुभानि).

Traditional interpretation

The prayer विदधातु शुभानि सा नः has an echo in the gods’ hymn of chapter 5 (5.81: करोतु सा नः शुभहेतुरीश्वरी शुभानि). The name Ambikā, ‘Mother’, is the one the text uses most often for the warrior Goddess.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) also has the name Ambikā (name 295, अम्बिका). The two works are separate; the name is shared Śākta usage.

Verse 4.4

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

यस्याः प्रभावमतुलं भगवाननन्तोब्रह्मा हरश्च न हि वक्तुमलं बलं च।सा चण्डिकाखिलजगत्परिपालनायनाशाय चाशुभभयस्य मतिं करोतु॥⁠४⁠॥

yasyāḥ prabhāvamatulaṃ bhagavānanantobrahmā haraśca na hi vaktumalaṃ balaṃ ca |sā caṇḍikākhilajagatparipālanāyanāśāya cāśubhabhayasya matiṃ karotu ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यस्याः · प्रभावम् · अतुलम् · भगवान् · अनन्तःब्रह्मा · हरः · च · न · हि · वक्तुम् · अलम् · बलम् · चसा · चण्डिका · अखिलजगत्परिपालनायनाशाय · च · अशुभभयस्य · मतिम् · करोतु

yasyāḥ · prabhāvam · atulam · bhagavān · anantaḥbrahmā · haraḥ · ca · na · hi · vaktum · alam · balam · casā · caṇḍikā · akhilajagatparipālanāyanāśāya · ca · aśubhabhayasya · matim · karotu

Word by word

  1. यस्याःyasyāḥwhoserelative pronoun यद्, f. gen. sg.
  2. प्रभावम्prabhāvammajesty, powerm. acc. sg. of प्रभाव; object of वक्तुम्
  3. अतुलम्atulamincomparablem. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies प्रभावम्
  4. भगवान्bhagavānthe Blessedm. nom. sg. of भगवत् (possessive -vat stem); qualifies अनन्तः
  5. अनन्तःanantaḥAnanta, the Endless One (Viṣṇu)m. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject
  6. ब्रह्माbrahmāBrahmām. nom. sg. of ब्रह्मन्; subject
  7. हरःharaḥHara (Śiva)m. nom. sg. of हर; subject
  8. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  9. नnanotindeclinable (negation)
  10. हिhiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  11. वक्तुम्vaktumto tell, to describeinfinitive (tumun) of √वच् (adādi, 2P ‘to speak’)
  12. अलम्alamable, sufficientindeclinable; with the infinitive ‘capable of (doing)’
  13. बलम्balamstrength, mightn. acc. sg. of बल; second object of वक्तुम्
  14. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); joins बलम् to प्रभावम्
  15. साsāshe, thatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; correlative of यस्याः
  16. चण्डिकाcaṇḍikāCaṇḍikā, the Fierce Onef. nom. sg. of चण्डिका; subject of करोतु
  17. अखिलजगत्परिपालनायakhilajagatparipālanāyafor the protection of the whole worldn. dat. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); dative of purpose
  18. नाशायnāśāyafor the destructionm. dat. sg. of नाश; dative of purpose
  19. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  20. अशुभभयस्यaśubhabhayasyaof evil and fearn. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on नाशाय
  21. मतिम्matimmind, resolvef. acc. sg. of मति; object of करोतु
  22. करोतुkarotumay she make, may she set√कृ (tanādi, 8U ‘to do’), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

Translation

Whose incomparable majesty and strength the Blessed Ananta, Brahmā and Hara are indeed not able to tell — may that Caṇḍikā set her mind on the protection of the whole world and on the destruction of evil and fear.

In readable EnglishHer incomparable majesty and strength not even blessed Viṣṇu, Brahmā or Śiva can describe. May that Caṇḍikā turn her mind to protecting the whole world and to ending evil and fear.

Ananta and Hara are named as Viṣṇu and Śiva; ‘not even’ renders the emphatic न हि.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • भगवाननन्तो bhagavānananto → भगवान् + अनन्तः

    final n before a vowel; visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    After the long ā the final n is not doubled (ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम्, 8.3.32, needs a short vowel); it is simply written with the vowel. अनन्तः before the voiced b of ब्रह्मा becomes अनन्तो.

  • हरश्च haraśca → हरः + च

    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    Visarga before c → s → ś.

  • वक्तुमलं बलं च vaktumalaṃ balaṃ ca → वक्तुम् + अलम् + बलम् + च

    final m before vowel and consonants · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    वक्तुम् keeps its m before the vowel of अलम्; अलम् and बलम् show anusvāra before b and c.

  • चण्डिकाखिल caṇḍikākhila → चण्डिका + अखिल…

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā. Likewise चाशुभ (च + अशुभ).

  • मतिं करोतु matiṃ karotu → मतिम् + करोतु

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant is written as anusvāra.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अतुलम् atulam

    nañ-bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अ + तुला
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते तुला यस्य तत् na vidyate tulā yasya tat
    Literally
    “that which has no balance (to weigh it against)”
    In the verse
    incomparable

  • अनन्तः anantaḥ

    nañ-bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अ + अन्त
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते अन्तः यस्य सः na vidyate antaḥ yasya saḥ
    Literally
    “he who has no end”
    In the verse
    Ananta — here Viṣṇu, beside Brahmā and Hara
    Alternative
    Ananta is also the name of Śeṣa, the serpent with a thousand mouths; the triad with Brahmā and Hara favours Viṣṇu.

  • अखिलजगत्परिपालनाय akhilajagatparipālanāya

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a karmadhāraya (अखिलजगत्)

    Members
    अखिल + जगत् + परिपालन
    Vigraha
    अखिलं जगत् अखिलजगत्; अखिलजगतः परिपालनम्, तस्मै akhilaṃ jagat akhilajagat; akhilajagataḥ paripālanam, tasmai
    Literally
    “for the protection of the whole world”
    In the verse
    to protect all that lives

  • अशुभभयस्य aśubhabhayasya

    samāhāra dvandva (neuter singular)

    Members
    अशुभ + भय
    Vigraha
    अशुभं च भयं च अशुभभयम्, तस्य aśubhaṃ ca bhayaṃ ca aśubhabhayam, tasya
    Literally
    “of evil and fear”
    In the verse
    of misfortune and dread
    Alternative
    Also read as a pañcamī tatpuruṣa, अशुभात् भयम्, ‘fear arising from evil’. The sense is close; the dvandva is the more common reading.

  • अशुभ aśubha

    nañ tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अ + शुभ
    Vigraha
    न शुभम् na śubham
    Literally
    “not auspicious”
    In the verse
    evil, misfortune
Morphology & anvaya

  • वक्तुम् vaktum

    Infinitive of √वच् (वच् + तुम्, c → k before t). With अलम् it expresses capacity: न … वक्तुम् अलम् ‘not able to tell’; the verb ‘are’ is understood.

  • करोतु karotu

    Imperative third singular of √कृ: stem करो- (tanādi u-class) + तु. मतिं करोतु ‘may she set her mind on’.

  • भगवान् bhagavān

    Nominative singular of भगवत् (भग + matup → -वत्): the vowel before the -nt is lengthened and the final t lost: भगवान्.

Anvaya · prose order

यस्याः अतुलं प्रभावं बलं च भगवान् अनन्तः ब्रह्मा हरः च वक्तुं न हि अलम्, सा चण्डिका अखिलजगत्परिपालनाय अशुभभयस्य नाशाय च मतिं करोतु।

yasyāḥ atulaṃ prabhāvaṃ balaṃ ca bhagavān anantaḥ brahmā haraḥ ca vaktuṃ na hi alam, sā caṇḍikā akhilajagatparipālanāya aśubhabhayasya nāśāya ca matiṃ karotu |

A relative clause (यस्याः … अलम्, the verb ‘are’ understood) and its correlative main clause (सा चण्डिका … करोतु); two datives of purpose depend on मतिं करोतु.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The three great gods of the triad cannot describe her power; the lesser gods therefore do not try to measure her but ask her to act. The prayer has two aims: protection (परिपालन) and the removal of evil and fear.

Traditional interpretation

The thought that even Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva cannot describe the Goddess is a commonplace of Śākta hymns; the next stanzas (4.6–4.7) develop it. Caṇḍikā, ‘the Fierce One’, is the name from which the whole text takes its popular title, the Caṇḍī.

Verse 4.5

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

या श्रीः स्वयं सुकृतिनां भवनेष्वलक्ष्मीःपापात्मनां कृतधियां हृदयेषु बुद्धिः।श्रद्धा सतां कुलजनप्रभवस्य लज्जातां त्वां नताः स्म परिपालय देवि विश्वम्॥⁠५⁠॥

yā śrīḥ svayaṃ sukṛtināṃ bhavaneṣvalakṣmīḥpāpātmanāṃ kṛtadhiyāṃ hṛdayeṣu buddhiḥ |śraddhā satāṃ kulajanaprabhavasya lajjātāṃ tvāṃ natāḥ sma paripālaya devi viśvam ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

या · श्रीः · स्वयम् · सुकृतिनाम् · भवनेषु · अलक्ष्मीःपापात्मनाम् · कृतधियाम् · हृदयेषु · बुद्धिःश्रद्धा · सताम् · कुलजनप्रभवस्य · लज्जाताम् · त्वाम् · नताः · स्म · परिपालय · देवि · विश्वम्

yā · śrīḥ · svayam · sukṛtinām · bhavaneṣu · alakṣmīḥpāpātmanām · kṛtadhiyām · hṛdayeṣu · buddhiḥśraddhā · satām · kulajanaprabhavasya · lajjātām · tvām · natāḥ · sma · paripālaya · devi · viśvam

Word by word

  1. याyā(you) whorelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.
  2. श्रीःśrīḥŚrī, good fortune, prosperityf. nom. sg. of श्री; predicate
  3. स्वयम्svayamyourself, in personindeclinable (reflexive adverb)
  4. सुकृतिनाम्sukṛtināmof the virtuous, of those who do goodm. gen. pl. of the in-stem सुकृतिन्
  5. भवनेषुbhavaneṣuin the homesn. loc. pl. of भवन
  6. अलक्ष्मीःalakṣmīḥAlakṣmī, misfortunef. nom. sg. (ī-stem with -s in the nominative); compound (see Samāsa); predicate
  7. पापात्मनाम्pāpātmanāmof the evil-souledm. gen. pl. of the an-stem पापात्मन्; compound (see Samāsa); ‘(in the homes) of’ is supplied from the first clause
  8. कृतधियाम्kṛtadhiyāmof those whose minds are cultivated, the wisem. gen. pl. of the root-noun stem कृतधी; compound (see Samāsa)
  9. हृदयेषुhṛdayeṣuin the heartsn. loc. pl. of हृदय
  10. बुद्धिःbuddhiḥunderstanding, intelligencef. nom. sg. of बुद्धि; predicate
  11. श्रद्धाśraddhāfaithf. nom. sg. of श्रद्धा; predicate
  12. सताम्satāmof the goodm. gen. pl. of सत्, present participle (śatṛ) of √अस् ‘to be’, used as a noun
  13. कुलजनप्रभवस्यkulajanaprabhavasyaof one born of a noble familym. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  14. लज्जाlajjāmodesty, the sense of shamef. nom. sg. of लज्जा; predicate
  15. ताम्tāmto that (one)pronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; correlative of या; object of नताः
  16. त्वाम्tvāmto yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, acc. sg.; in apposition to ताम्
  17. नताःnatāḥbowedpast participle (kta) of √नम् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to bow’), m. nom. pl.
  18. स्मsma(we) areas printed the indeclinable स्म; understood as स्मः ‘we are’ (√अस्, laṭ, uttama-puruṣa bahuvacana) without its visarga, as in 4.3
  19. परिपालयparipālayaprotectपरि + √पाल् (curādi, 10P ‘to guard’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  20. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  21. विश्वम्viśvamthe universen. acc. sg. of विश्व; object of परिपालय

Translation

You who are yourself Śrī in the homes of the virtuous, Alakṣmī (in those) of the evil-souled, understanding in the hearts of those of cultivated mind, faith of the good, modesty of one born of a noble family — to that you we are bowed. O Devī, protect the universe.

In readable EnglishYou are Fortune herself in the homes of the virtuous and Misfortune in the homes of the wicked; understanding in the hearts of the wise, faith in the good, modesty in the well-born. To you we bow: O Devī, protect the world.

‘Homes’ is supplied for the wicked from the first clause; ‘in the good’, ‘in the well-born’ render genitives.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • भवनेष्वलक्ष्मीः bhavaneṣvalakṣmīḥ → भवनेषु + अलक्ष्मीः

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    Final u before a dissimilar vowel becomes v.

  • पापात्मनां कृतधियां हृदयेषु pāpātmanāṃ kṛtadhiyāṃ hṛdayeṣu → पापात्मनाम् + कृतधियाम् + हृदयेषु

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    The final m of both genitive plurals is written as anusvāra before a consonant. Likewise सतां कुल…, तां त्वां.

  • पापात्मनां pāpātmanāṃ → पाप + आत्मनाम्

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā.

  • श्रीः स्वयं śrīḥ svayaṃ → श्रीः + स्वयम्

    visarga before a sibilant · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)

    The visarga is kept before s (assimilation to स्स् is the optional alternative).

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अलक्ष्मीः alakṣmīḥ

    nañ tatpuruṣa (in the sense of the opposite)

    Members
    अ + लक्ष्मी
    Vigraha
    न लक्ष्मीः na lakṣmīḥ
    Literally
    “not-Lakṣmī”
    In the verse
    misfortune, Lakṣmī’s opposite

  • पापात्मनाम् pāpātmanām

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    पाप + आत्मन्
    Vigraha
    पापः आत्मा येषां ते, तेषाम् pāpaḥ ātmā yeṣāṃ te, teṣām
    Literally
    “of those whose self is sinful”
    In the verse
    of the wicked

  • कृतधियाम् kṛtadhiyām

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    कृत + धी
    Vigraha
    कृता (संस्कृता) धीः येषां ते, तेषाम् kṛtā (saṃskṛtā) dhīḥ yeṣāṃ te, teṣām
    Literally
    “of those whose understanding is made (formed)”
    In the verse
    of the cultivated, the learned

  • कुलजनप्रभवस्य kulajanaprabhavasya

    bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (कुलजन)

    Members
    कुलजन + प्रभव
    Vigraha
    कुलस्य (सत्कुलस्य) जनाः कुलजनाः; कुलजनाः प्रभवः यस्य सः, तस्य kulasya (satkulasya) janāḥ kulajanāḥ; kulajanāḥ prabhavaḥ yasya saḥ, tasya
    Literally
    “of one whose origin is the people of a family”
    In the verse
    of the well-born
    Alternative
    कुल here has the sense ‘good family’, as in कुलीन. The compound may also be taken as a pañcamī-type bahuvrīhi, कुलजनेभ्यः प्रभवः यस्य; the sense is unchanged.
Morphology & anvaya

  • सुकृतिनाम् sukṛtinām

    सुकृतिन् is a possessive in -in from सुकृत ‘good deed’: ‘one who has good deeds’. Genitive plural -इनाम्.

  • कृतधियाम् kṛtadhiyām

    धी ‘thought’ at the end of a compound declines as a root-noun: before a vowel ending ī becomes iy (कृतधियाम्).

  • सताम् satām

    Present participle of √अस् (सत्, the a of the root lost), genitive plural: ‘of those who are (truly)’, i.e. the good.

  • परिपालय paripālaya

    Imperative second singular of the curādi stem पालय-: the bare stem serves as the form. The hymn turns from description to request.

Anvaya · prose order

या (त्वं) सुकृतिनां भवनेषु स्वयं श्रीः, पापात्मनां (भवनेषु) अलक्ष्मीः, कृतधियां हृदयेषु बुद्धिः, सतां श्रद्धा, कुलजनप्रभवस्य लज्जा (असि), तां त्वां (वयं) नताः स्म। देवि, विश्वं परिपालय।

yā (tvaṃ) sukṛtināṃ bhavaneṣu svayaṃ śrīḥ, pāpātmanāṃ (bhavaneṣu) alakṣmīḥ, kṛtadhiyāṃ hṛdayeṣu buddhiḥ, satāṃ śraddhā, kulajanaprabhavasya lajjā (asi), tāṃ tvāṃ (vayaṃ) natāḥ sma | devi, viśvaṃ paripālaya |

Five predicates in a relative clause (या … लज्जा, the verb ‘are’ understood), each with a genitive of the persons in whom she is that quality; the correlative ताम् त्वाम् is the object of bowing; a closing imperative.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

One Devī is named in five qualities, each placed in a kind of person: prosperity and its opposite, understanding, faith and modesty. The same power that blesses the virtuous is misfortune in the house of the wicked; the stanza does not make her two goddesses.

Traditional interpretation

Several of these names return as forms of the Devī ‘established in all beings’ in the hymn of chapter 5: बुद्धिरूपेण (5.20-22), लज्जारूपेण (5.44-46), श्रद्धारूपेण (5.50-52), लक्ष्मीरूपेण (5.56-58). Brahmā’s hymn had already named her श्री and बुद्धि (1.79).

Modern reflection

A modern reading: the stanza places the divine in a household’s fortune, a mind’s clarity, a person’s faith and restraint — the Goddess is met in character as much as in temples.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) includes Lajjā among the Goddess’s names (name 740, लज्जा).

Verse 4.6

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

किं वर्णयाम तव रूपमचिन्त्यमेतत्किं चातिवीर्यमसुरक्षयकारि भूरि।किं चाहवेषु चरितानि तवाद्भुतानिसर्वेषु देव्यसुरदेवगणादिकेषु॥⁠६⁠॥

kiṃ varṇayāma tava rūpamacintyametatkiṃ cātivīryamasurakṣayakāri bhūri |kiṃ cāhaveṣu caritāni tavādbhutānisarveṣu devyasuradevagaṇādikeṣu ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

किम् · वर्णयाम · तव · रूपम् · अचिन्त्यम् · एतत्किम् · च · अतिवीर्यम् · असुरक्षयकारि · भूरिकिम् · च · आहवेषु · चरितानि · तव · अद्भुतानिसर्वेषु · देवि · असुरदेवगणादिकेषु

kim · varṇayāma · tava · rūpam · acintyam · etatkim · ca · ativīryam · asurakṣayakāri · bhūrikim · ca · āhaveṣu · caritāni · tava · adbhutānisarveṣu · devi · asuradevagaṇādikeṣu

Word by word

  1. किम्kimhow? what?interrogative pronoun किम्, n. acc. sg., used adverbially
  2. वर्णयामvarṇayāmashould we describe√वर्ण् (curādi, 10P ‘to describe, depict’), loṭ (imperative), uttama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada; deliberative
  3. तवtavayoursecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, gen. sg.
  4. रूपम्rūpamformn. acc. sg. of रूप; object of वर्णयाम
  5. अचिन्त्यम्acintyambeyond thought, unthinkablen. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
  6. एतत्etatthispronoun एतद्, n. acc. sg.; qualifies रूपम्
  7. किम्kimhow?interrogative pronoun, n. acc. sg., adverbial
  8. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  9. अतिवीर्यम्ativīryamsurpassing valourn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of वर्णयाम (understood)
  10. असुरक्षयकारिasurakṣayakārithat brings destruction to the asurasn. acc. sg. of the in-stem असुरक्षयकारिन्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अतिवीर्यम्
  11. भूरिbhūriabundant, greatadjective भूरि, n. acc. sg.; qualifies अतिवीर्यम्
  12. किम्kimhow?interrogative pronoun, n. acc. sg., adverbial
  13. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  14. आहवेषुāhaveṣuin battlesm. loc. pl. of आहव
  15. चरितानिcaritānideedsn. acc. pl. of चरित (kta of √चर् used as a noun); object of वर्णयाम (understood)
  16. तवtavayoursecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, gen. sg.
  17. अद्भुतानिadbhutāniwondrousn. acc. pl. of अद्भुत; qualifies चरितानि
  18. सर्वेषुsarveṣuamong allpronominal adjective सर्व, m. loc. pl.; qualifies असुरदेवगणादिकेषु
  19. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  20. असुरदेवगणादिकेषुasuradevagaṇādikeṣuthe hosts of asuras, gods and the restm. loc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa)

Translation

How should we describe this unthinkable form of yours? And (how) the abundant, surpassing valour that destroys the asuras? And (how), O Devī, your wondrous deeds in battles, among all the hosts of asuras, gods and the rest?

In readable EnglishHow can we describe this form of yours, beyond all thought? Or your boundless valour that destroys the asuras? Or the wonders you have done in battle, O Devī, among all the hosts of gods, asuras and others?

Textual verification / uncertain: The locative सर्वेषु … असुरदेवगणादिकेषु can be read ‘(in battles) among all the hosts of asuras, gods and others’ or, as a locative of reference, ‘(wondrous) to all the hosts …’. The first is followed here.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • रूपमचिन्त्यमेतत् rūpamacintyametat → रूपम् + अचिन्त्यम् + एतत्

    final m before a vowel · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    Both m’s stand before vowels and are written together with them.

  • चातिवीर्यमसुरक्षयकारि cātivīryamasurakṣayakāri → च + अतिवीर्यम् + असुरक्षयकारि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha); final m before a vowel · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā; the m of अतिवीर्यम् is written with the following a. Likewise चाहवेषु (च + आहवेषु) and तवाद्भुतानि (तव + अद्भुतानि).

  • देव्यसुर devyasura → देवि + असुर…

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    The final i of the vocative becomes y before a.

  • किं वर्णयाम kiṃ varṇayāma → किम् + वर्णयाम

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; likewise किं च in lines 2 and 3.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • अचिन्त्यम् acintyam

    nañ tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अ + चिन्त्य
    Vigraha
    न चिन्त्यम् na cintyam
    Literally
    “not to be thought”
    In the verse
    beyond the reach of thought

  • अतिवीर्यम् ativīryam

    prādi tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अति + वीर्य
    Vigraha
    अतिशयितं वीर्यम् atiśayitaṃ vīryam
    Literally
    “excessive valour”
    In the verse
    valour beyond all measure

  • असुरक्षयकारि asurakṣayakāri

    upapada tatpuruṣa (agent noun कारिन्, kṛt -in) containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (असुरक्षय)

    Members
    असुर + क्षय + कारिन्
    Vigraha
    असुराणां क्षयः असुरक्षयः; तं करोति इति असुरक्षयकारि asurāṇāṃ kṣayaḥ asurakṣayaḥ; taṃ karoti iti asurakṣayakāri
    Literally
    “making the destruction of the asuras”
    In the verse
    that wiped out the demons

  • असुरदेवगणादिकेषु asuradevagaṇādikeṣu

    bahuvrīhi (with आदि + svārthe -क) containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa and an itaretara dvandva (असुरदेव)

    Members
    असुर + देव + गण + आदिक
    Vigraha
    असुराः च देवाः च असुरदेवाः; तेषां गणाः असुरदेवगणाः; ते आदयः येषां ते असुरदेवगणादिकाः, तेषु asurāḥ ca devāḥ ca asuradevāḥ; teṣāṃ gaṇāḥ asuradevagaṇāḥ; te ādayaḥ yeṣāṃ te asuradevagaṇādikāḥ, teṣu
    Literally
    “among those beginning with the hosts of asuras and gods”
    In the verse
    among gods, demons and all other beings
Morphology & anvaya

  • वर्णयाम varṇayāma

    Imperative first plural of the curādi stem वर्णय-: the first-person imperative (‘let us …’) with किम् asks a deliberative question — ‘how are we to describe?’

  • असुरक्षयकारि asurakṣayakāri

    Neuter accusative singular of an -in stem: कारिन् → कारि (the n is dropped in the nominative/accusative neuter). It agrees with अतिवीर्यम्.

  • अचिन्त्यम् acintyam

    Gerundive (yat) of √चिन्त् ‘to think’ with the negative: ‘not to be thought’.

Anvaya · prose order

(वयं) तव एतत् अचिन्त्यं रूपं किं वर्णयाम? असुरक्षयकारि भूरि अतिवीर्यं च किम्? देवि, सर्वेषु असुरदेवगणादिकेषु आहवेषु तव अद्भुतानि चरितानि च किम् (वर्णयाम)?

(vayaṃ) tava etat acintyaṃ rūpaṃ kiṃ varṇayāma? asurakṣayakāri bhūri ativīryaṃ ca kim? devi, sarveṣu asuradevagaṇādikeṣu āhaveṣu tava adbhutāni caritāni ca kim (varṇayāma)?

Three questions with one verb (वर्णयाम), each introduced by किम्: the form, the valour, the deeds. The locative सर्वेषु … असुरदेवगणादिकेषु is taken as ‘among all the hosts’ (the battles fought among them).

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The gods confess that their praise cannot match its object: three questions, each beginning with किम्, about her form, her valour and her deeds. The hymn returns to the same three — form (रूप), valour (वीर्य) and conduct — in 4.21.

Traditional interpretation

The confession of inadequacy is a convention of Sanskrit hymns, but here it follows directly on 4.4: if Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva cannot describe her power, the lesser gods can only wonder at it.

Modern reflection

A modern reading: worship begins where description fails. The questions are not a refusal to speak but a way of speaking that keeps the mystery open.

Verse 4.7

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

हेतुः समस्तजगतां त्रिगुणापि दोषै-र्न ज्ञायसे हरिहरादिभिरप्यपारा।सर्वाश्रयाखिलमिदं जगदंशभूत-मव्याकृता हि परमा प्रकृतिस्त्वमाद्या॥⁠७⁠॥

hetuḥ samastajagatāṃ triguṇāpi doṣai-rna jñāyase hariharādibhirapyapārā |sarvāśrayākhilamidaṃ jagadaṃśabhūta-mavyākṛtā hi paramā prakṛtistvamādyā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

हेतुः · समस्तजगताम् · त्रिगुणा · अपि · दोषैःन · ज्ञायसे · हरिहरादिभिः · अपि · अपारासर्वाश्रया · अखिलम् · इदम् · जगत् · अंशभूतम्अव्याकृता · हि · परमा · प्रकृतिः · त्वम् · आद्या

hetuḥ · samastajagatām · triguṇā · api · doṣaiḥna · jñāyase · hariharādibhiḥ · api · apārāsarvāśrayā · akhilam · idam · jagat · aṃśabhūtamavyākṛtā · hi · paramā · prakṛtiḥ · tvam · ādyā

Word by word

  1. हेतुःhetuḥthe causem. nom. sg. of हेतु (u-stem); predicate of the implied त्वम् (a masculine noun predicated of the Goddess)
  2. समस्तजगताम्samastajagatāmof all the worldsn. gen. pl.; compound (see Samāsa)
  3. त्रिगुणाtriguṇāpossessed of the three guṇasf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  4. अपिapithoughindeclinable (concessive)
  5. दोषैःdoṣaiḥby faults, by defectsm. instr. pl. of दोष; with the passive ज्ञायसे
  6. नnanotindeclinable (negation)
  7. ज्ञायसेjñāyaseyou are known√ज्ञा (kryādi, 9U ‘to know’), karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana
  8. हरिहरादिभिःhariharādibhiḥby Hari, Hara and the othersm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa)
  9. अपिapievenindeclinable (emphatic)
  10. अपाराapārāwithout a farther shore, unfathomablef. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  11. सर्वाश्रयाsarvāśrayāthe support of allf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  12. अखिलम्akhilamwholen. nom. sg. of अखिल; qualifies जगत्
  13. इदम्idamthispronoun इदम्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies जगत्
  14. जगत्jagatworldn. nom. sg.; subject
  15. अंशभूतम्aṃśabhūtamis a portion (of you)n. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of जगत्
  16. अव्याकृताavyākṛtāthe unmanifest, undifferentiatedf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies प्रकृतिः
  17. हिhifor, indeedindeclinable (causal particle)
  18. परमाparamāsupremef. nom. sg. of परम; qualifies प्रकृतिः
  19. प्रकृतिःprakṛtiḥPrakṛti, primordial naturef. nom. sg. of प्रकृति; predicate of त्वम्
  20. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.
  21. आद्याādyāprimal, firstf. nom. sg. of आद्य (आदि + yat); qualifies प्रकृतिः

Translation

(You are) the cause of all the worlds; though possessed of the three guṇas, you are not known by faults; (you are) unfathomable even by Hari, Hara and the others; the support of all — this whole world is (your) portion; for you are the unmanifest, supreme, primal Prakṛti.

In readable EnglishYou are the cause of all the worlds. Though you bear the three guṇas, no fault of theirs is found in you; even Viṣṇu, Śiva and the other gods cannot reach your farther shore. You are the support of all, and this whole world is but a portion of you, for you are the primal Prakṛti, supreme and unmanifest.

‘No fault of theirs is found in you’ follows the usual reading of दोषैः न ज्ञायसे (‘you are not known (to be touched) by faults’); see flags for the other.

Textual verification / uncertain: दोषैः न ज्ञायसे admits two readings: ‘you are not known (to be touched) by faults’ (the usual one, followed in tr) or, with दोषैः as the agent of the passive, ‘you are not known by (those who have) faults’. Both are grammatical.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • त्रिगुणापि triguṇāpi → त्रिगुणा + अपि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • दोषैर्न doṣairna → दोषैः + न

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After ai the visarga (as रु) is sounded r before the voiced n. The printed text breaks the line between दोषै- and र्न; the word दोषैः belongs to the first line.

  • हरिहरादिभिरप्यपारा hariharādibhirapyapārā → हरिहरादिभिः + अपि + अपारा

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); इको यणचि (6.1.77)

    -iḥ before a vowel is sounded r; the i of अपि becomes y before a.

  • सर्वाश्रयाखिलमिदं sarvāśrayākhilamidaṃ → सर्वाश्रया + अखिलम् + इदम्

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha); final m · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    ā + a → ā; अखिलम् keeps its m before the vowel of इदम्, and इदम् shows anusvāra before j.

  • जगदंशभूत jagadaṃśabhūta → जगत् + अंशभूतम्

    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    Final t becomes d before a vowel. अंशभूतम् runs over the line-end (…भूत- / मव्याकृता) and keeps its m before the vowel of अव्याकृता.

  • प्रकृतिस्त्वमाद्या prakṛtistvamādyā → प्रकृतिः + त्वम् + आद्या

    visarga sandhi; final m before a vowel · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)

    Visarga before t becomes s; the m of त्वम् is written with the following ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • समस्तजगताम् samastajagatām

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    समस्त + जगत्
    Vigraha
    समस्तानि जगन्ति, तेषाम् samastāni jaganti, teṣām
    Literally
    “of the entire worlds”
    In the verse
    of every world

  • त्रिगुणा triguṇā

    bahuvrīhi (with a numeral first member)

    Members
    त्रि + गुण
    Vigraha
    त्रयः गुणाः यस्याः सा trayaḥ guṇāḥ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she who has three guṇas”
    In the verse
    made of sattva, rajas and tamas

  • हरिहरादिभिः hariharādibhiḥ

    bahuvrīhi containing an itaretara dvandva (हरिहरौ)

    Members
    हरि + हर + आदि
    Vigraha
    हरिः च हरः च हरिहरौ; हरिहरौ आदी येषां ते, तैः hariḥ ca haraḥ ca hariharau; hariharau ādī yeṣāṃ te, taiḥ
    Literally
    “by those of whom Hari and Hara are first”
    In the verse
    by Viṣṇu, Śiva and the other gods

  • अपारा apārā

    nañ-bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अ + पार
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते पारः यस्याः सा na vidyate pāraḥ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she who has no farther shore”
    In the verse
    boundless, beyond being crossed or fathomed

  • सर्वाश्रया sarvāśrayā

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, made feminine to agree with the Goddess

    Members
    सर्व + आश्रय
    Vigraha
    सर्वस्य आश्रयः (सर्वाश्रयभूता) sarvasya āśrayaḥ (sarvāśrayabhūtā)
    Literally
    “the resting-place of all”
    In the verse
    that on which everything depends

  • अंशभूतम् aṃśabhūtam

    karmadhāraya (भूत ‘being, become’ as the second member: ‘being a part’)

    Members
    अंश + भूत
    Vigraha
    अंशः भूतम् (अंशरूपम्) aṃśaḥ bhūtam (aṃśarūpam)
    Literally
    “become a part”
    In the verse
    a mere portion (of you)

  • अव्याकृता avyākṛtā

    nañ tatpuruṣa

    Members
    अ + व्याकृत
    Vigraha
    न व्याकृता na vyākṛtā
    Literally
    “not made distinct”
    In the verse
    the unmanifest, before name and form
Morphology & anvaya

  • ज्ञायसे jñāyase

    Passive present of √ज्ञा: stem ज्ञाय- (ज्ञा + yak) with the ātmanepada ending -से, second singular: ‘you are known’. The agent of a passive is put in the instrumental, so दोषैः can be read as agent (see flags).

  • अव्याकृता avyākṛtā

    Past participle of वि + आ + √कृ (व्याकृत ‘separated out, made distinct’) with the negative अ-: the Sāṃkhya-Vedānta term for the undifferentiated cause before creation.

  • आद्या ādyā

    आद्य is आदि ‘beginning’ + the taddhita -य (the final i dropped): ‘being at the beginning, primal’; feminine आद्या.

Anvaya · prose order

(त्वं) समस्तजगतां हेतुः (असि)। त्रिगुणा अपि दोषैः न ज्ञायसे। हरिहरादिभिः अपि अपारा (असि)। सर्वाश्रया (असि); इदम् अखिलं जगत् (तव) अंशभूतम्; त्वं हि आद्या परमा अव्याकृता प्रकृतिः (असि)।

(tvaṃ) samastajagatāṃ hetuḥ (asi) | triguṇā api doṣaiḥ na jñāyase | hariharādibhiḥ api apārā (asi) | sarvāśrayā (asi); idam akhilaṃ jagat (tava) aṃśabhūtam; tvaṃ hi ādyā paramā avyākṛtā prakṛtiḥ (asi) |

A string of predicates of an unexpressed त्वम्, one finite verb (ज्ञायसे, passive), a nominal clause about the world (जगत् अंशभूतम्), and the reason, introduced by हि: you are the primal Prakṛti.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The hymn turns from her deeds to her nature: she is the cause of the worlds and the primal, unmanifest Prakṛti, of which the world is only a part. The paradox of the first line: she has the three guṇas, yet their faults do not cling to her. Even Hari and Hara do not reach her farther shore.

Traditional interpretation

The vocabulary is Sāṃkhya (prakṛti, the three guṇas, avyākṛta), but the Prakṛti of this hymn is not an unconscious matter set against spirit: she is the Goddess herself, the cause and support of all. Brahmā’s hymn says the same (1.78: प्रकृतिस्त्वं च सर्वस्य गुणत्रयविभाविनी).

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) names the Goddess मूलप्रकृतिः (name 397) and त्रिगुणा (name 984) — the same two ideas in a different text.

Verse 4.8

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

यस्याः समस्तसुरता समुदीरणेनतृप्तिं प्रयाति सकलेषु मखेषु देवि।स्वाहासि वै पितृगणस्य च तृप्तिहेतु-रुच्चार्यसे त्वमत एव जनैः स्वधा च॥⁠८⁠॥

yasyāḥ samastasuratā samudīraṇenatṛptiṃ prayāti sakaleṣu makheṣu devi |svāhāsi vai pitṛgaṇasya ca tṛptihetu-ruccāryase tvamata eva janaiḥ svadhā ca ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

यस्याः · समस्तसुरता · समुदीरणेनतृप्तिम् · प्रयाति · सकलेषु · मखेषु · देविस्वाहा · असि · वै · पितृगणस्य · च · तृप्तिहेतुःउच्चार्यसे · त्वम् · अतः · एव · जनैः · स्वधा · च

yasyāḥ · samastasuratā · samudīraṇenatṛptim · prayāti · sakaleṣu · makheṣu · devisvāhā · asi · vai · pitṛgaṇasya · ca · tṛptihetuḥuccāryase · tvam · ataḥ · eva · janaiḥ · svadhā · ca

Word by word

  1. यस्याःyasyāḥof whom (whose)relative pronoun यद्, f. gen. sg.; objective genitive with समुदीरणेन
  2. समस्तसुरताsamastasuratāthe whole company of the godsf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of प्रयाति
  3. समुदीरणेनsamudīraṇenaby the utteringn. instr. sg. of समुदीरण (सम् + उत् + √ईर्, kṛt -ana ‘the act of’)
  4. तृप्तिम्tṛptimsatisfactionf. acc. sg. of तृप्ति; object of प्रयाति
  5. प्रयातिprayātiattains, goes toप्र + √या (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  6. सकलेषुsakaleṣuin allm. loc. pl. of सकल; qualifies मखेषु
  7. मखेषुmakheṣuin sacrificesm. loc. pl. of मख
  8. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  9. स्वाहाsvāhāSvāhā, the call with which offerings are made to the godsthe ritual indeclinable स्वाहा, used here as a feminine name: f. nom. sg.; predicate
  10. असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi, 2P ‘to be’), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  11. वैvaiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  12. पितृगणस्यpitṛgaṇasyaof the host of ancestorsm. gen. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on तृप्तिहेतुः
  13. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  14. तृप्तिहेतुःtṛptihetuḥthe cause of satisfactionm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to त्वम्
  15. उच्चार्यसेuccāryaseyou are pronounced, you are utteredउद् + √चर् (bhvādi, 1P ‘to move’), causative (उच्चारय- ‘to pronounce’), karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ, madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana
  16. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of the passive
  17. अतःataḥthereforeindeclinable (adverb of cause, from इदम्)
  18. एवevaprecisely, for that very (reason)indeclinable (restrictive particle)
  19. जनैःjanaiḥby peoplem. instr. pl. of जन; agent of the passive
  20. स्वधाsvadhāSvadhā, the call with which offerings are made to the ancestorsthe ritual indeclinable स्वधा, used as a feminine name: f. nom. sg.; predicate with उच्चार्यसे
  21. चcaand, alsoindeclinable (conjunction)

Translation

O Devī, you by whose utterance in all sacrifices the whole company of the gods attains satisfaction — you are indeed Svāhā; and, being the cause of the satisfaction of the host of ancestors, for that very reason you are pronounced by people as Svadhā too.

In readable EnglishO Devī, at every sacrifice it is by the uttering of you that all the gods together are satisfied: you are Svāhā. And because you satisfy the host of the ancestors, people utter you as Svadhā.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • तृप्तिं प्रयाति tṛptiṃ prayāti → तृप्तिम् + प्रयाति

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra.

  • स्वाहासि svāhāsi → स्वाहा + असि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • तृप्तिहेतुरुच्चार्यसे tṛptiheturuccāryase → तृप्तिहेतुः + उच्चार्यसे

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After u the visarga (as रु) is sounded r before a vowel. The line is broken between तृप्तिहेतु- and रुच्चार्यसे; the word तृप्तिहेतुः belongs to the third line.

  • त्वमत एव tvamata eva → त्वम् + अतः + एव

    final m before a vowel; visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)

    त्वम् keeps its m before a; -aḥ before a vowel other than a becomes y, the y is dropped, and अत | एव stand apart.

  • उच्चार्यसे uccāryase → उत् + चार्यसे

    consonant sandhi with a verbal prefix · स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40)

    The t of the prefix उत् becomes c beside the palatal c.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • समस्तसुरता samastasuratā

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    समस्त + सुरता
    Vigraha
    समस्ता सुरता samastā suratā
    Literally
    “the whole god-assembly”
    In the verse
    all the gods together

  • पितृगणस्य pitṛgaṇasya

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    पितृ + गण
    Vigraha
    पितॄणां गणः, तस्य pitṝṇāṃ gaṇaḥ, tasya
    Literally
    “of the host of the fathers”
    In the verse
    of the ancestors, to whom śrāddha offerings are made

  • तृप्तिहेतुः tṛptihetuḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    तृप्ति + हेतु
    Vigraha
    तृप्तेः हेतुः tṛpteḥ hetuḥ
    Literally
    “cause of satisfaction”
    In the verse
    she who satisfies them
Morphology & anvaya

  • समस्तसुरता samastasuratā

    सुरता is सुर ‘god’ with the taddhita -ता in the collective sense (‘a body of gods’), formed like जनता ‘a body of people’.

  • उच्चार्यसे uccāryase

    Passive of the causative: उत् + चारि (√चर् + ṇic) + yak + से; the causative’s -i- is lost before the passive -य-. ‘You are made to sound, are pronounced’.

  • स्वाहा svāhā

    स्वाहा and स्वधा are indeclinable ritual words, the calls that accompany offerings to the gods and to the ancestors. The hymn treats them as names of the Goddess and makes them predicates (‘you are Svāhā’).

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, सकलेषु मखेषु यस्याः समुदीरणेन समस्तसुरता तृप्तिं प्रयाति, (सा त्वं) स्वाहा असि वै। पितृगणस्य च तृप्तिहेतुः (त्वम्), अतः एव त्वं जनैः स्वधा च उच्चार्यसे।

devi, sakaleṣu makheṣu yasyāḥ samudīraṇena samastasuratā tṛptiṃ prayāti, (sā tvaṃ) svāhā asi vai | pitṛgaṇasya ca tṛptihetuḥ (tvam), ataḥ eva tvaṃ janaiḥ svadhā ca uccāryase |

A relative clause (यस्याः … प्रयाति) and its main clause (स्वाहा असि वै), then a second sentence with a passive verb whose agent is जनैः and whose predicate nominative is स्वधा.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Svāhā is spoken as an oblation is poured into the fire for the gods; Svadhā when it is offered to the ancestors. The hymn identifies the Goddess with these sacred words themselves: the gods and the ancestors are fed through her.

Traditional interpretation

Brahmā’s hymn in chapter 1 already said त्वं स्वाहा त्वं स्वधा (1.73). Here the identification is explained: she is Svāhā because the gods are satisfied by her utterance, Svadhā because the ancestors are.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has the same pair of names side by side: स्वाहा (name 535) and स्वधा (name 536).

Verse 4.9

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

या मुक्तिहेतुरविचिन्त्यमहाव्रता त्व-मभ्यस्यसे सुनियतेन्द्रियतत्त्वसारैः।मोक्षार्थिभिर्मुनिभिरस्तसमस्तदोषै-र्विद्यासि सा भगवती परमा हि देवि॥⁠९⁠॥

yā muktiheturavicintyamahāvratā tva-mabhyasyase suniyatendriyatattvasāraiḥ |mokṣārthibhirmunibhirastasamastadoṣai-rvidyāsi sā bhagavatī paramā hi devi ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

या · मुक्तिहेतुः · अविचिन्त्यमहाव्रता · त्वम्अभ्यस्यसे · सुनियतेन्द्रियतत्त्वसारैःमोक्षार्थिभिः · मुनिभिः · अस्तसमस्तदोषैःविद्या · असि · सा · भगवती · परमा · हि · देवि

yā · muktihetuḥ · avicintyamahāvratā · tvamabhyasyase · suniyatendriyatattvasāraiḥmokṣārthibhiḥ · munibhiḥ · astasamastadoṣaiḥvidyā · asi · sā · bhagavatī · paramā · hi · devi

Word by word

  1. याyā(you) whorelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.
  2. मुक्तिहेतुःmuktihetuḥthe cause of liberationm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate
  3. अविचिन्त्यमहाव्रताavicintyamahāvratāwhose great vow is beyond thoughtf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  4. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of अभ्यस्यसे
  5. अभ्यस्यसेabhyasyaseyou are practised, constantly dwelt uponअभि + √अस् (divādi, 4P ‘to throw’; with अभि ‘to practise, repeat’), karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ, madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana
  6. सुनियतेन्द्रियतत्त्वसारैःsuniyatendriyatattvasāraiḥwith senses well restrained, holding reality as the essencem. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies मुनिभिः
  7. मोक्षार्थिभिःmokṣārthibhiḥby seekers of liberationm. instr. pl. of the in-stem मोक्षार्थिन्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies मुनिभिः
  8. मुनिभिःmunibhiḥby sagesm. instr. pl. of मुनि; agent of the passive अभ्यस्यसे
  9. अस्तसमस्तदोषैःastasamastadoṣaiḥwho have cast off all faultsm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies मुनिभिः
  10. विद्याvidyāknowledge, the liberating wisdomf. nom. sg. of विद्या; predicate
  11. असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi, 2P), laṭ, madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  12. साsāthatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; correlative of या
  13. भगवतीbhagavatīblessedf. nom. sg. of भगवती (feminine of भगवत्); qualifies विद्या
  14. परमाparamāsupremef. nom. sg. of परम; qualifies विद्या
  15. हिhiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
  16. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी

Translation

You who are the cause of liberation, whose great vow is unthinkable, who are practised by sages seeking liberation, their senses well restrained, holding reality as the essence, all faults cast off — you are that supreme, blessed Knowledge, O Devī.

In readable EnglishYou are the cause of liberation, your great discipline beyond thought. Sages who seek release — senses held in check, intent on the truth, every fault cast off — make you their practice. You, O Devī, are that blessed and supreme Knowledge.

‘Make you their practice’ turns the passive अभ्यस्यसे (‘you are practised’) active.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • मुक्तिहेतुरविचिन्त्य muktiheturavicintya → मुक्तिहेतुः + अविचिन्त्य…

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After u the visarga is sounded r before a vowel.

  • त्वमभ्यस्यसे tvamabhyasyase → त्वम् + अभ्यस्यसे

    final m before a vowel · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    The line breaks between त्व- and मभ्यस्यसे; त्वम् belongs to the first line and its m is joined to the vowel of the next word.

  • सुनियतेन्द्रिय suniyatendriya → सुनियत + इन्द्रिय

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + i → e.

  • मोक्षार्थिभिर्मुनिभिरस्त mokṣārthibhirmunibhirasta → मोक्षार्थिभिः + मुनिभिः + अस्त…

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After i the visarga is sounded r before the voiced m and before the vowel a.

  • दोषैर्विद्यासि doṣairvidyāsi → दोषैः + विद्या + असि

    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    After ai the visarga is r before v (the line breaks after दोषै-); ā + a → ā.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • मुक्तिहेतुः muktihetuḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    मुक्ति + हेतु
    Vigraha
    मुक्तेः हेतुः mukteḥ hetuḥ
    Literally
    “cause of release”
    In the verse
    she who brings liberation

  • अविचिन्त्यमहाव्रता avicintyamahāvratā

    bahuvrīhi containing karmadhārayas (अविचिन्त्यमहाव्रत; महाव्रत = महत् व्रतम्)

    Members
    अविचिन्त्य + महाव्रत
    Vigraha
    अविचिन्त्यं महाव्रतं यस्याः सा avicintyaṃ mahāvrataṃ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she whose great vow is unthinkable”
    In the verse
    she whose great observance is beyond thought
    Alternative
    The feminine ending (व्रत is neuter) shows a bahuvrīhi. Whether the ‘great vow’ is the Goddess’s own or the discipline by which the sages reach her is left open by the compound; the next words (अभ्यस्यसे … मुनिभिः) favour the second.

  • सुनियतेन्द्रियतत्त्वसारैः suniyatendriyatattvasāraiḥ

    karmadhāraya of two bahuvrīhis

    Members
    सुनियत + इन्द्रिय + तत्त्व + सार
    Vigraha
    सुनियतानि इन्द्रियाणि येषां ते सुनियतेन्द्रियाः; तत्त्वं सारः येषां ते तत्त्वसाराः; सुनियतेन्द्रियाः च ते तत्त्वसाराः च, तैः suniyatāni indriyāṇi yeṣāṃ te suniyatendriyāḥ; tattvaṃ sāraḥ yeṣāṃ te tattvasārāḥ; suniyatendriyāḥ ca te tattvasārāḥ ca, taiḥ
    Literally
    “by those whose senses are well restrained and whose essence (chief concern) is reality”
    In the verse
    disciplined, intent on the truth
    Alternative
    Also read as a single bahuvrīhi, सुनियतेन्द्रियत्वम् एव तत्त्वसारः येषाम् ‘for whom the restraint of the senses is the heart of the matter’; the first analysis is simpler.

  • मोक्षार्थिभिः mokṣārthibhiḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    मोक्ष + अर्थिन्
    Vigraha
    मोक्षस्य अर्थिनः, तैः mokṣasya arthinaḥ, taiḥ
    Literally
    “by seekers of release”
    In the verse
    by those who desire liberation
    Alternative
    Also upapada: मोक्षम् अर्थयन्ते इति मोक्षार्थिनः.

  • अस्तसमस्तदोषैः astasamastadoṣaiḥ

    bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अस्त + समस्त + दोष
    Vigraha
    अस्ताः समस्ताः दोषाः यैः ते, तैः astāḥ samastāḥ doṣāḥ yaiḥ te, taiḥ
    Literally
    “by those by whom all faults are thrown off”
    In the verse
    free of every flaw
Morphology & anvaya

  • अभ्यस्यसे abhyasyase

    Passive present of अभि + √अस् ‘to throw’ (divādi अस्यति): with अभि it means ‘to practise, repeat, apply oneself to’. The agent is मुनिभिः: the Goddess is the object of the sages’ discipline.

  • मोक्षार्थिभिः mokṣārthibhiḥ

    Instrumental plural of the in-stem अर्थिन् ‘one who seeks’ (from अर्थ ‘aim’): -इभिः.

  • विद्या vidyā

    With the correlative सा and the adjectives परमा, भगवती, the predicate picks up 1.57 (सा विद्या परमा मुक्तेर्हेतुभूता सनातनी).

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, या त्वं मुक्तिहेतुः अविचिन्त्यमहाव्रता (असि), सुनियतेन्द्रियतत्त्वसारैः अस्तसमस्तदोषैः मोक्षार्थिभिः मुनिभिः अभ्यस्यसे, सा (त्वं) परमा भगवती विद्या असि हि।

devi, yā tvaṃ muktihetuḥ avicintyamahāvratā (asi), suniyatendriyatattvasāraiḥ astasamastadoṣaiḥ mokṣārthibhiḥ munibhiḥ abhyasyase, sā (tvaṃ) paramā bhagavatī vidyā asi hi |

A relative clause (या … अभ्यस्यसे) with two predicates and a passive verb whose agent is मुनिभिः and its three epithets; the main clause is सा … विद्या असि.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

After the ritual words of 4.8 the hymn names the Goddess as the goal of contemplative practice: the knowledge (vidyā) that liberates, pursued by disciplined sages.

Traditional interpretation

The stanza echoes Medhas’s teaching in chapter 1: सा विद्या परमा मुक्तेर्हेतुभूता सनातनी (1.57), and the gods’ hymn in chapter 11 calls her मुक्तिहेतुः again (11.5). The same Goddess who binds as Mahāmāyā (1.55) is the knowledge that releases.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) also names the Goddess विद्या (name 549) and मुक्तिरूपिणी (name 737).

Verse 4.10

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

शब्दात्मिका सुविमलर्ग्यजुषां निधान-मुद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवतां च साम्नाम्।देवी त्रयी भगवती भवभावनायवार्ता च सर्वजगतां परमार्तिहन्त्री॥⁠१०⁠॥

śabdātmikā suvimalargyajuṣāṃ nidhāna-mudgītharamyapadapāṭhavatāṃ ca sāmnām |devī trayī bhagavatī bhavabhāvanāyavārtā ca sarvajagatāṃ paramārtihantrī ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

शब्दात्मिका · सुविमलर्ग्यजुषाम् · निधानम्उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवताम् · च · साम्नाम्देवी · त्रयी · भगवती · भवभावनायवार्ता · च · सर्वजगताम् · परमार्तिहन्त्री

śabdātmikā · suvimalargyajuṣām · nidhānamudgītharamyapadapāṭhavatām · ca · sāmnāmdevī · trayī · bhagavatī · bhavabhāvanāyavārtā · ca · sarvajagatām · paramārtihantrī

Word by word

  1. शब्दात्मिकाśabdātmikāwhose very self is sound (the sacred word)f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  2. सुविमलर्ग्यजुषाम्suvimalargyajuṣāmof the very pure Ṛk and Yajus (verses)n. gen. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); depends on निधानम्
  3. निधानम्nidhānamthe repository, treasure-housen. nom. sg. of निधान (नि + √धा + kṛt -ana); predicate
  4. उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवताम्udgītharamyapadapāṭhavatāmwhich have a recitation of words made delightful by the udgīthan. gen. pl. of a possessive in -vat (matup); compound (see Samāsa); qualifies साम्नाम्
  5. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  6. साम्नाम्sāmnāmof the Sāmans (chants)n. gen. pl. of सामन्; depends on निधानम्
  7. देवीdevīthe Devīf. nom. sg. of देवी
  8. त्रयीtrayīthe Triple (Veda)f. nom. sg. of त्रयी (the threefold Veda: Ṛk, Yajus, Sāman); predicate
  9. भगवतीbhagavatīthe Blessed Onef. nom. sg. of भगवती
  10. भवभावनायbhavabhāvanāyafor bringing the world into being and sustaining itn. dat. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); dative of purpose
  11. वार्ताvārtāVārttā, the science of livelihood (agriculture, cattle-keeping, trade)f. nom. sg. of वार्ता; predicate
  12. चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
  13. सर्वजगताम्sarvajagatāmof all the worldsn. gen. pl.; compound (see Samāsa)
  14. परमार्तिहन्त्रीparamārtihantrīdestroyer of the greatest distressf. nom. sg. of the agent noun हन्त्री (हन्तृ + ṅīp); compound (see Samāsa)

Translation

Having sound as your self, you are the repository of the very pure Ṛk and Yajus and of the Sāmans, which possess a recitation of words delightful with the udgītha. You, the Blessed Devī, are the Triple (Veda) for the bringing-forth of the world, and Vārttā, the destroyer of the supreme distress of all the worlds.

In readable EnglishYour very self is sound: you are the treasure-house of the spotless Ṛk and Yajus and of the Sāman chants, lovely in their words with the udgītha. Blessed Devī, you are the Triple Veda, for the sustaining of the world, and you are Vārttā, the livelihood that ends the deepest distress of all the worlds.

Vārttā, a technical name, is glossed as ‘livelihood’ (agriculture, cattle and trade); ‘sustaining’ interprets भावन ‘causing to be’.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • शब्दात्मिका śabdātmikā → शब्द + आत्मिका

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + ā → ā. Likewise परमार्ति (परम + आर्ति).

  • सुविमलर्ग्यजुषां suvimalargyajuṣāṃ → सुविमल + ऋक् + यजुषाम्

    vowel sandhi (guṇa) and consonant sandhi (voicing) inside a compound · आद्गुणः (6.1.87); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    a + ṛ → ar; the stem ऋच् ends in k when it stands before another member, and the k is voiced to g before y. The final m becomes anusvāra before n (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

  • निधानमुद्गीथ nidhānamudgītha → निधानम् + उद्गीथ…

    final m before a vowel · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    The line breaks between निधान- and मुद्गीथ…; निधानम् belongs to the first line and its m joins the vowel of the next word.

  • उद्गीथ udgītha → उत् + गीथ

    consonant sandhi with a prefix (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)

    The t of उत् is voiced to d before g.

  • पाठवतां च pāṭhavatāṃ ca → पाठवताम् + च

    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; likewise सर्वजगतां परमार्ति…

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • शब्दात्मिका śabdātmikā

    bahuvrīhi (feminine -इका from आत्मक)

    Members
    शब्द + आत्मन् (+ -क)
    Vigraha
    शब्दः आत्मा यस्याः सा śabdaḥ ātmā yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she whose self is sound”
    In the verse
    the Goddess as the Word, the substance of the Veda

  • सुविमलर्ग्यजुषाम् suvimalargyajuṣām

    karmadhāraya containing an itaretara dvandva (ऋग्यजुस्); सुविमल is a prādi compound (सुष्ठु विमलम्)

    Members
    सुविमल + ऋच् + यजुस्
    Vigraha
    ऋचः च यजूंषि च ऋग्यजूंषि; सुविमलानि ऋग्यजूंषि, तेषाम् ṛcaḥ ca yajūṃṣi ca ṛgyajūṃṣi; suvimalāni ṛgyajūṃṣi, teṣām
    Literally
    “of the very pure Ṛc-verses and Yajus-formulas”
    In the verse
    of the Ṛgveda and the Yajurveda

  • उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवताम् udgītharamyapadapāṭhavatām

    possessive (matup) on a karmadhāraya that contains tatpuruṣas (tṛtīyā उद्गीथरम्य, ṣaṣṭhī पदपाठ)

    Members
    उद्गीथ + रम्य + पद + पाठ + -वत्
    Vigraha
    पदानां पाठः पदपाठः; उद्गीथेन रम्यः पदपाठः उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठः; सः एषाम् अस्ति इति उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवन्ति, तेषाम् padānāṃ pāṭhaḥ padapāṭhaḥ; udgīthena ramyaḥ padapāṭhaḥ udgītharamyapadapāṭhaḥ; saḥ eṣām asti iti udgītharamyapadapāṭhavanti, teṣām
    Literally
    “possessing a recitation of words delightful with the udgītha”
    In the verse
    the Sāman chants, sung with the udgītha

  • भवभावनाय bhavabhāvanāya

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa

    Members
    भव + भावन
    Vigraha
    भवस्य भावनम्, तस्मै bhavasya bhāvanam, tasmai
    Literally
    “for the causing-to-be of the world”
    In the verse
    for the world’s coming into being and welfare

  • सर्वजगताम् sarvajagatām

    karmadhāraya

    Members
    सर्व + जगत्
    Vigraha
    सर्वाणि जगन्ति, तेषाम् sarvāṇi jaganti, teṣām
    Literally
    “of all worlds”
    In the verse
    of every world

  • परमार्तिहन्त्री paramārtihantrī

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing a karmadhāraya (परमार्ति)

    Members
    परम + आर्ति + हन्त्री
    Vigraha
    परमा आर्तिः परमार्तिः; परमार्तेः हन्त्री paramā ārtiḥ paramārtiḥ; paramārteḥ hantrī
    Literally
    “slayer of the supreme pain”
    In the verse
    she who ends the worst distress
    Alternative
    परम may also qualify the whole (‘the supreme destroyer of distress’); the karmadhāraya परमा आर्तिः is the natural reading.
Morphology & anvaya

  • साम्नाम् sāmnām

    Genitive plural of the n-stem सामन्: the a of -man is dropped before the ending (साम्नाम्).

  • परमार्तिहन्त्री paramārtihantrī

    हन्तृ is the agent noun of √हन् (kṛt tṛc); its feminine takes ṅīp: हन्त्री ‘she who slays’. In the compound the outside genitive सर्वजगताम् goes with आर्ति: ‘the distress of all worlds’.

  • उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवताम् udgītharamyapadapāṭhavatām

    Genitive plural of a possessive stem in -वत् (matup): -वताम्. It agrees with साम्नाम्.

Anvaya · prose order

(त्वं) शब्दात्मिका सुविमलर्ग्यजुषाम् उद्गीथरम्यपदपाठवतां साम्नां च निधानम् (असि)। भगवती देवी (त्वं) भवभावनाय त्रयी (असि), सर्वजगतां परमार्तिहन्त्री वार्ता च (असि)।

(tvaṃ) śabdātmikā suvimalargyajuṣām udgītharamyapadapāṭhavatāṃ sāmnāṃ ca nidhānam (asi) | bhagavatī devī (tvaṃ) bhavabhāvanāya trayī (asi), sarvajagatāṃ paramārtihantrī vārtā ca (asi) |

Nominal sentences with the verb ‘are’ understood. भवभावनाय is taken with त्रयी (the Veda for the world’s sustenance); it may also go with both त्रयी and वार्ता.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

The Goddess is the Veda as sound: the Ṛk, the Yajus and the sung Sāman, whose central chant is the udgītha. She is also Vārttā, the knowledge by which people earn their living: the sacred and the practical are both her forms.

Traditional interpretation

Trayī (the threefold Veda) and Vārttā (agriculture, cattle-rearing and trade) are two of the four branches of knowledge in the classical list known from the Arthaśāstra, with ānvīkṣikī (reasoning) and daṇḍanīti (government). Brahmā’s hymn had called her स्वरात्मिका, ‘whose self is the sounded syllable’ (1.73); here she is शब्दात्मिका.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) names the Goddess त्रयी (name 872).

Verse 4.11

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

मेधासि देवि विदिताखिलशास्त्रसारादुर्गासि दुर्गभवसागरनौरसङ्गा।श्रीः कैटभारिहृदयैककृताधिवासागौरी त्वमेव शशिमौलिकृतप्रतिष्ठा॥⁠११⁠॥

medhāsi devi viditākhilaśāstrasārādurgāsi durgabhavasāgaranaurasaṅgā |śrīḥ kaiṭabhārihṛdayaikakṛtādhivāsāgaurī tvameva śaśimaulikṛtapratiṣṭhā ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

मेधा · असि · देवि · विदिताखिलशास्त्रसारादुर्गा · असि · दुर्गभवसागरनौः · असङ्गाश्रीः · कैटभारिहृदयैककृताधिवासागौरी · त्वम् · एव · शशिमौलिकृतप्रतिष्ठा

medhā · asi · devi · viditākhilaśāstrasārādurgā · asi · durgabhavasāgaranauḥ · asaṅgāśrīḥ · kaiṭabhārihṛdayaikakṛtādhivāsāgaurī · tvam · eva · śaśimaulikṛtapratiṣṭhā

Word by word

  1. मेधाmedhāMedhā, intelligence that grasps and retainsf. nom. sg. of मेधा; predicate
  2. असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi, 2P ‘to be’), laṭ, madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  3. देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
  4. विदिताखिलशास्त्रसाराviditākhilaśāstrasārāby whom the essence of all the śāstras is knownf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies मेधा
  5. दुर्गाdurgāDurgā, the Hard-to-reachf. nom. sg. of दुर्गा; predicate
  6. असिasiyou are√अस्, laṭ, madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
  7. दुर्गभवसागरनौःdurgabhavasāgaranauḥthe boat across the hard-to-cross ocean of worldly existencef. nom. sg. of the au-stem नौ at the end of a compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to दुर्गा
  8. असङ्गाasaṅgāunattachedf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
  9. श्रीःśrīḥŚrī (Lakṣmī)f. nom. sg. of श्री; predicate
  10. कैटभारिहृदयैककृताधिवासाkaiṭabhārihṛdayaikakṛtādhivāsāwho has made her dwelling in the heart of Kaiṭabha’s foe alonef. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies श्रीः
  11. गौरीgaurīGaurī, the Fair One (Pārvatī)f. nom. sg. of गौरी; predicate
  12. त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
  13. एवevaindeed, you aloneindeclinable (restrictive particle)
  14. शशिमौलिकृतप्रतिष्ठाśaśimaulikṛtapratiṣṭhāwho has her place established with the moon-crested one (Śiva)f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies गौरी

Translation

You are Medhā, O Devī, by whom the essence of all the śāstras is known; you are Durgā, the boat across the hard-to-cross ocean of existence, unattached; Śrī, who has made her dwelling in the heart of Kaiṭabha’s foe alone; you alone are Gaurī, who has made her place in the moon-crested one.

In readable EnglishO Devī, you are Medhā, who knows the essence of every scripture; you are Durgā, the boat across the perilous ocean of worldly life, yourself untouched by attachment. You are Śrī, whose one dwelling is the heart of Viṣṇu, Kaiṭabha’s foe; and you alone are Gaurī, established with the moon-crowned Śiva.

Kaiṭabha’s foe and the moon-crested one are named as Viṣṇu and Śiva.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • मेधासि medhāsi → मेधा + असि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā; the same junction gives दुर्गासि.

  • विदिताखिलशास्त्रसारा viditākhilaśāstrasārā → विदित + अखिल + शास्त्र + सारा

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā. Likewise कैटभारि (कैटभ + अरि) and कृताधिवासा (कृत + अधिवासा).

  • नौरसङ्गा naurasaṅgā → नौः + असङ्गा

    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)

    After au the visarga (as रु) is sounded r before a vowel.

  • हृदयैक hṛdayaika → हृदय + एक

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)

    a + e → ai.

  • त्वमेव tvameva → त्वम् + एव

    final m before a vowel · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) applies only before a consonant

    The m is written together with the following vowel.

Samāsa & samāsa-vigraha

  • विदिताखिलशास्त्रसारा viditākhilaśāstrasārā

    bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (…शास्त्रसार) and a karmadhāraya (अखिलशास्त्र)

    Members
    विदित + अखिल + शास्त्र + सार
    Vigraha
    अखिलानि शास्त्राणि अखिलशास्त्राणि; तेषां सारः अखिलशास्त्रसारः; विदितः अखिलशास्त्रसारः यया सा akhilāni śāstrāṇi akhilaśāstrāṇi; teṣāṃ sāraḥ akhilaśāstrasāraḥ; viditaḥ akhilaśāstrasāraḥ yayā sā
    Literally
    “she by whom the essence of all treatises is known”
    In the verse
    the intelligence that has grasped the heart of all learning

  • दुर्गभवसागरनौः durgabhavasāgaranauḥ

    ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa containing karmadhārayas (दुर्गभवसागर; भवसागर, a rūpaka: the world as an ocean)

    Members
    दुर्ग + भवसागर + नौ
    Vigraha
    भवः एव सागरः भवसागरः; दुर्गः भवसागरः दुर्गभवसागरः; तस्य नौः bhavaḥ eva sāgaraḥ bhavasāgaraḥ; durgaḥ bhavasāgaraḥ durgabhavasāgaraḥ; tasya nauḥ
    Literally
    “the boat of the hard-to-cross ocean of becoming”
    In the verse
    the boat that carries one across saṃsāra

  • असङ्गा asaṅgā

    nañ-bahuvrīhi

    Members
    अ + सङ्ग
    Vigraha
    न विद्यते सङ्गः यस्याः सा na vidyate saṅgaḥ yasyāḥ sā
    Literally
    “she who has no attachment”
    In the verse
    untouched by what she carries across

  • कैटभारिहृदयैककृताधिवासा kaiṭabhārihṛdayaikakṛtādhivāsā

    bahuvrīhi containing a karmadhāraya (कृताधिवास) and ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣas (कैटभारि, कैटभारिहृदय)

    Members
    कैटभारि + हृदय + एक + कृत + अधिवास
    Vigraha
    कैटभस्य अरिः कैटभारिः; कैटभारेः हृदयं कैटभारिहृदयम्; तद् एव एकम् (स्थानम्) कैटभारिहृदयैकम्; तस्मिन् कृतः अधिवासः यया सा kaiṭabhasya ariḥ kaiṭabhāriḥ; kaiṭabhāreḥ hṛdayaṃ kaiṭabhārihṛdayam; tad eva ekam (sthānam) kaiṭabhārihṛdayaikam; tasmin kṛtaḥ adhivāsaḥ yayā sā
    Literally
    “she by whom a dwelling has been made in the one (place), the heart of Kaiṭabha’s foe”
    In the verse
    Lakṣmī, who lives in Viṣṇu’s heart alone

  • शशिमौलिकृतप्रतिष्ठा śaśimaulikṛtapratiṣṭhā

    bahuvrīhi containing a vyadhikaraṇa bahuvrīhi (शशिमौलि)

    Members
    शशिमौलि + कृत + प्रतिष्ठा
    Vigraha
    शशी मौलौ यस्य सः शशिमौलिः; शशिमौलौ कृता प्रतिष्ठा यया सा śaśī maulau yasya saḥ śaśimauliḥ; śaśimaulau kṛtā pratiṣṭhā yayā sā
    Literally
    “she by whom a standing-place has been made in the moon-crested one”
    In the verse
    Gaurī, established as Śiva’s consort
    Alternative
    Also read with शशिमौलि as agent, शशिमौलिना कृता प्रतिष्ठा यस्याः सा, ‘she who has been given her place (honoured) by Śiva’. The parallel with कृताधिवासा in the line before favours the first.
Morphology & anvaya

  • दुर्गभवसागरनौः durgabhavasāgaranauḥ

    नौ ‘boat’ is an au-stem: nominative singular नौः, which keeps its form at the end of the compound. Before the vowel of असङ्गा its visarga is sounded r.

  • दुर्गा durgā

    A play on words: दुर्गा ‘the hard-to-reach’ is the boat across the दुर्ग, the hard-to-cross, ocean of existence. दुर्ग is दुर् + √गम् (‘hard to go’).

  • असि asi

    Present second singular of √अस्: the root’s final s is dropped before the ending -सि (अस् + सि → असि). Repeated in the first two lines, understood in the last two.

Anvaya · prose order

देवि, (त्वं) विदिताखिलशास्त्रसारा मेधा असि; असङ्गा दुर्गभवसागरनौः दुर्गा असि; कैटभारिहृदयैककृताधिवासा श्रीः (असि); शशिमौलिकृतप्रतिष्ठा गौरी (च) त्वम् एव (असि)।

devi, (tvaṃ) viditākhilaśāstrasārā medhā asi; asaṅgā durgabhavasāgaranauḥ durgā asi; kaiṭabhārihṛdayaikakṛtādhivāsā śrīḥ (asi); śaśimaulikṛtapratiṣṭhā gaurī (ca) tvam eva (asi) |

Four identifications, each name with its epithet: Medhā, Durgā, Śrī, Gaurī. त्वम् एव in the last line sums them up: you alone are all of them.

Śākta commentary

Direct textual meaning

Four goddesses are named — Medhā (learning), Durgā, Śrī (Lakṣmī, consort of Viṣṇu, who killed Kaiṭabha in chapter 1) and Gaurī (consort of Śiva) — and the last line says that the Devī alone is all of them. The word-play दुर्गा / दुर्ग makes Durgā the boat across the ocean that is hard to cross.

Traditional interpretation

The Devī herself later gives another account of the name Durgā: she will slay the asura Durgama and be known as Durgā (11.50). The stanza is often read as a statement of the Śākta teaching that the consorts of the gods are forms of the one Goddess.

Comparative Śrīvidyā notes

Comparative Śākta / Śrīvidyā

Lalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has three of these names: मेधा (name 538), दुर्गा (name 190) and गौरी (name 635).

Verse 4.12

Śloka · vasantatilakā
‹›

ईषत्सहासममलं परिपूर्णचन्द्र-बिम्बानुकारि कनकोत्तमकान्तिकान्तम्।अत्यद्भुतं प्रहृतमात्तरुषा तथापिवक्त्रं विलोक्य सहसा महिषासुरेण॥⁠१२⁠॥

īṣatsahāsamamalaṃ paripūrṇacandra-bimbānukāri kanakottamakāntikāntam |atyadbhutaṃ prahṛtamāttaruṣā tathāpivaktraṃ vilokya sahasā mahiṣāsureṇa ||

Padaccheda · the words separated

ईषत्सहासम् · अमलम् · परिपूर्णचन्द्रबिम्बानुकारिकनकोत्तमकान्तिकान्तम्अत्यद्भुतम् · प्रहृतम् · आत्तरुषा · तथा · अपिवक्त्रम् · विलोक्य · सहसा · महिषासुरेण

īṣatsahāsam · amalam · paripūrṇacandrabimbānukārikanakottamakāntikāntamatyadbhutam · prahṛtam · āttaruṣā · tathā · apivaktram · vilokya · sahasā · mahiṣāsureṇa

Word by word

  1. ईषत्सहासम्īṣatsahāsamgently smilingn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वक्त्रम्
  2. अमलम्amalamspotlessn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वक्त्रम्
  3. परिपूर्णचन्द्रबिम्बानुकारिparipūrṇacandrabimbānukāriresembling the orb of the full moonn. acc. sg. of the in-stem …अनुकारिन्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वक्त्रम्
  4. कनकोत्तमकान्तिकान्तम्kanakottamakāntikāntamlovely with the lustre of the finest goldn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies वक्त्रम्
  5. अत्यद्भुतम्atyadbhutamexceedingly wonderfuln. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of the whole statement
  6. प्रहृतम्prahṛtam(that) a blow was struckpast passive participle (kta) of प्र + √हृ (bhvādi, 1U ‘to carry’; with प्र ‘to strike’), n. nom. sg., bhāve (impersonal) prayoga
  7. आत्तरुषाāttaruṣāseized with wrathm. instr. sg. of the consonant stem आत्तरुष्; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महिषासुरेण
  8. तथाtathāsoindeclinable (adverb of manner)
  9. अपिapievenindeclinable; with तथा, ‘even so, nevertheless’
  10. वक्त्रम्vaktram(your) facen. acc. sg. of वक्त्र; object of विलोक्य
  11. विलोक्यvilokyahaving looked atabsolutive (lyap) of वि + √लोक् (‘to see’)
  12. सहसाsahasāsuddenly, rashlyindeclinable (adverb; instrumental of सहस् in form)
  13. महिषासुरेणmahiṣāsureṇaby Mahiṣāsuram. instr. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); agent of प्रहृतम्

Translation

Having looked at (your) face — gently smiling, spotless, resembling the orb of the full moon, lovely with the lustre of the finest gold — that even so a blow was suddenly struck by Mahiṣāsura, seized with wrath, (is) exceedingly wonderful.

In readable EnglishYour face was gently smiling and spotless, like the orb of the full moon, lovely with the glow of finest gold; that Mahiṣāsura could look on it and even so, seized by fury, strike out at once — that is a great wonder.

‘Your’ is supplied (the face is the Devī’s); the impersonal passive is turned active.

Sandhi & sandhi-viccheda

  • ईषत्सहासममलं īṣatsahāsamamalaṃ → ईषत्सहासम् + अमलम्

    final m before a vowel and before a consonant · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    ईषत्सहासम् keeps its m before the vowel of अमलम्; अमलम् shows anusvāra before p.

  • परिपूर्णचन्द्रबिम्बानुकारि paripūrṇacandrabimbānukāri → परिपूर्ण + चन्द्र + बिम्ब + अनुकारि

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā. The compound runs over the line-end (…चन्द्र- / बिम्बानुकारि) and belongs to the first line.

  • कनकोत्तम kanakottama → कनक + उत्तम

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)

    a + u → o.

  • अत्यद्भुतं प्रहृतमात्तरुषा atyadbhutaṃ prahṛtamāttaruṣā → अति + अद्भुतम् + प्रहृतम् + आत्तरुषा

    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) inside a compound; final m · इको यणचि (6.1.77); मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)

    The i of अति becomes y before a; अत्यद्भुतम् shows anusvāra before p; प्रहृतम् keeps its m before the vowel ā.

  • तथापि tathāpi → तथा + अपि

    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    ā + a → ā.

  • महिषासुरेण mahiṣāsureṇa → महिष + असुरेन

    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha); retroflexion · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)

    a + a → ā; the n of the ending -ena becomes ṇ after r and ṣ in the same word with no dental or palatal between (ṇatva).

    Literally
    “being with a slight laugh”
    In the verse
    softly smiling

    In the verse
